```
{"result":"The Nix updater in this repository automates the process of updating Nix flake inputs, which in turn updates packages and system configurations. The primary mechanism involves GitHub Actions workflows that discover outdated flake inputs, update them, and create pull requests. There are also local Nix commands for manual updates.\n\n## Update Mechanisms\n\n### 1. GitHub Actions Workflows \nThe core of the automated updating process is handled by a reusable GitHub Actions workflow, `_update-flake-reusable.yaml` . This workflow is called by other workflows, such as `update-flake.yaml`  for stable updates and `update-flake-frequent.yaml` (not provided in snippets, but mentioned in wiki) for frequent updates.\n\nThe `_update-flake-reusable.yaml` workflow consists of three main jobs:\n*   **`discover`**: This job uses the `discover-flake-inputs` action  to identify flake inputs that need updating. It generates a JSON matrix of inputs to be updated .\n    *   The `discover-flake-inputs` action  reads the `flake.lock` file  and `nix flake metadata --json`  to get information about current flake inputs. It can be configured to update specific inputs, exclude others, or skip the release age check for certain inputs .\n*   **`update`**: This job runs for each discovered input that needs updating . It utilizes the `update-flake-input` action .\n    *   The `update-flake-input` action  performs the actual `nix flake update` command . It includes logic to check the minimum release age of an input before updating, unless `skip-delay` is set to `true` . For specific inputs like `llm-agents`, it also extracts and compares package versions to include in the PR title . After updating, it stages `flake.lock` , creates a new branch, commits the changes, and opens a pull request . It can also enable auto-merge for the created PR .\n*   **`summary`**: This job generates a summary of the update process .\n\n### 2. Auto-rebase Workflow \nThe `auto-rebase.yaml` workflow  automatically rebases open pull requests that were created by the flake update process. This ensures that update PRs stay up-to-date with the `main` branch . It triggers on pushes to `main` or manually via `workflow_dispatch` .\n\n### 3. Manual Nix Commands \nThe `flake.nix` file defines several `nix run .#` applications for manual updates and system management:\n*   `nix run .#update`: This command executes `nix flake update` to update all flake inputs .\n*   `nix run .#update-ai-tools`: This specifically updates the `llm-agents` flake input .\n*   `nix run .#switch`: After updating, this command builds and applies the new system configuration (either `nix-darwin` for macOS or `home-manager` for Linux) .\n*   `nix run .#build`: This command performs a dry run build of the configuration without applying it .\n\n### 4. Git Hooks \nThe repository also includes `post-merge` and `post-rewrite` Git hooks . These hooks automatically run `nix run .#switch` if changes are detected in `flake.nix`, `flake.lock`, or the `nix/` directory after a merge or rebase  .\n\n## Notes\nThe `update-node-packages.yaml` workflow  is a separate update mechanism specifically for npm packages used by Neovim, not directly related to Nix flake inputs. It runs `bash nix/packages/node/update.sh`  and creates a PR if changes are detected .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Package Overlays and Custom Packages (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#2.6)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

完璧です。リポジトリの構造を把握できました。調査結果をまとめます。

## dotnix リポジトリ構造調査結果

### 1. flake.nix 構造

**入力（inputs）の主要カテゴリ**:
- **システム構築**: `nixpkgs`, `home-manager`, `nix-darwin`, `flake-parts`
- **外部ツール**: `ghostty`, `niri`, `llm-agents`, `treefmt-nix`, `git-hooks`
- **GitHub Extensions**: `gh-nippou`, `gh-graph`
- **IM/入力**: `nix-hazkey`
- **スキル管理**: `agent-skills`, `anthropic-skills`, `vercel-skills`, `ui-ux-pro-max-skill`, `ast-grep-skill`
- **macOS**: `nix-darwin`, `onepassword-shell-plugins`, `brew-nix`
- **ユーティリティ**: `moonbit-overlay`, `nix-filter`, `version-lsp`

**出力（outputs）**:
- `nixosConfigurations.nixos` (x86_64-linux, GUI profile)
- `darwinConfigurations.darwin` (aarch64-darwin)
- Apps: `build`, `switch`, `fmt`

**switch/build コマンド**:
- `nix run .#switch` - Darwin or NixOS システムに適用
  - Darwin: `darwin-rebuild switch --flake .#darwin`
  - NixOS: `sudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .#nixos`
- `nix run .#build` - ビルドのみ（適用なし）
- `nix run .#fmt` - treefmt でコード整形

---

### 2. .github ディレクトリ構造

**Action / Workflow の階層構成**:

```
.github/
├── actions/
│   ├── setup-nix/              # Nix + numtide cache をセットアップ
│   ├── setup-git-bot/          # GitHub App トークン生成 + git identity
│   ├── discover-flake-inputs/  # flake.lock パース → 更新対象を検出
│   └── update-flake-input/     # 単一入力を更新 → PR 作成 → auto-merge
└── workflows/
    ├── _update-flake-reusable.yaml  # 再利用可能なワークフロー
    ├── update-flake.yaml            # 安定版: 毎日6時, 3日以上経過の入力を更新
    └── update-flake-frequent.yaml   # 頻繁版: 3時間ごと, llm-agents のみ
```

---

### 3. 既存の更新メカニズム

**2つの戦略**:

1. **update-flake.yaml** (安定版更新)
   - **スケジュール**: 毎日 UTC 6:00
   - **対象**: 全入力 EXCEPT `llm-agents`
   - **条件**: リリース後3日以上経過したコミット
   - **目的**: 破壊的変更を最小化

2. **update-flake-frequent.yaml** (頻繁更新)
   - **スケジュール**: 3時間ごと
   - **対象**: `llm-agents` のみ
   - **条件**: 任意のコミット
   - **目的**: AI ツール（claude-code, codex等）を常に最新に保つ

**update-flake-input アクション の処理フロー**:
1. ランダムディレイ（レート制限対策）
2. 現在/更新後のリビジョン取得
3. `nix flake update <input>`実行
4. リリース年齢チェック（スキップ可能）
5. 差分確認
6. `llm-agents`の場合のみ、パッケージ版情報テーブルを生成
7. ブランチ `update-flake-<input>` を作成 → コミット & プッシュ
8. PR 作成/更新
9. **自動マージ有効化**（`--auto --squash`）

---

### 4. 重要な詳細

**リビジョン管理**:
- `flake.lock`から `jq` で現在のリビジョン抽出
- GitHub API で更新後のコミット日時を確認
- 最小年齢チェックで古いリリースを除外

**PR自動マージ**:
```bash
gh pr merge <number> --auto --squash
```
- Squash マージで履歴をクリーンに保持

**セキュリティ**:
- GitHub App トークン（期間限定）を使用
- Git-secrets フック でシークレット検出
- ボットアイデンティティで自動化を識別

**カスタマイズポイント**:
- `exclude-inputs` / `include-inputs`: 入力のフィルタリング
- `skip-delay`: ランダムディレイのスキップ
- `minimum-release-age-days`: リリース年齢の最小期間

---

### 5. ファイルパス一覧

- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix`
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/.github/workflows/update-flake.yaml`
- `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/.github/workflows/update-flake-frequent.yaml`

この構造は既に非常に洗練されており、段階的な更新戦略（頻繁/安定）、自動マージ、バージョン表示などが実装されています。

```
     1→name: Discover Flake Inputs
     2→description: Parse flake metadata and output matrix of inputs to update
     3→
     4→inputs:
     5→  exclude-inputs:
     6→    description: Comma-separated list of inputs to exclude
     7→    default: ""
     8→  include-inputs:
     9→    description: Comma-separated list of inputs to include (if set, only these are included)
    10→    default: ""
    11→  skip-delay:
    12→    description: Skip random delay between updates
    13→    default: "false"
    14→
    15→outputs:
    16→  matrix:
    17→    description: JSON matrix of flake inputs
    18→    value: ${{ steps.discover.outputs.matrix }}
    19→
    20→runs:
    21→  using: composite
    22→  steps:
    23→    - id: discover
    24→      shell: bash
    25→      run: |
    26→        METADATA=$(nix flake metadata --json 2>/dev/null)
    27→
    28→        MATRIX=$(echo "$METADATA" | jq -c \
    29→          --arg include "${{ inputs.include-inputs }}" \
    30→          --arg exclude "${{ inputs.exclude-inputs }}" \
    31→          --arg skip_delay "${{ inputs.skip-delay }}" \
    32→          '
    33→          .locks.nodes as $nodes |
    34→          [
    35→            .locks.nodes.root.inputs | to_entries[] |
    36→            . as $entry |
    37→            $nodes[$entry.value] |
    38→            select((.locked.type == "github") or (.original.type == "github")) |
    39→            {
    40→              input: $entry.key,
    41→              owner: (.original.owner // .locked.owner),
    42→              repo: (.original.repo // .locked.repo)
    43→            }
    44→          ] |
    45→          if ($include | length) > 0 then
    46→            map(select(.input | IN($include | split(",") | map(gsub("\\s+"; ""))[])))
    47→          else . end |
    48→          if ($exclude | length) > 0 then
    49→            map(select(.input | IN($exclude | split(",") | map(gsub("\\s+"; ""))[]) | not))
    50→          else . end |
    51→          map(. + {skip_delay: ($skip_delay == "true")})
    52→          ')
    53→
    54→        echo "matrix={\"include\":${MATRIX}}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    55→        echo "Discovered inputs: $(echo "$MATRIX" | jq -r '[.[].input] | join(", ")')"
    56→
```

```
     1→name: Update Flake Input
     2→description: Update a single flake input, create PR, and enable auto-merge
     3→
     4→inputs:
     5→  input:
     6→    description: Flake input name
     7→    required: true
     8→  owner:
     9→    description: GitHub repository owner
    10→    required: true
    11→  repo:
    12→    description: GitHub repository name
    13→    required: true
    14→  token:
    15→    description: GitHub token for PR operations
    16→    required: true
    17→  skip-delay:
    18→    description: Skip random delay
    19→    default: "false"
    20→  minimum-release-age-days:
    21→    description: Minimum age in days for the release commit
    22→    default: "0"
    23→
    24→runs:
    25→  using: composite
    26→  steps:
    27→    - name: Random delay
    28→      if: inputs.skip-delay != 'true'
    29→      shell: bash
    30→      run: |
    31→        DELAY=$((RANDOM % 60))
    32→        echo "Sleeping ${DELAY}s to avoid rate limits"
    33→        sleep "$DELAY"
    34→
    35→    - name: Get current revision
    36→      id: current
    37→      shell: bash
    38→      run: |
    39→        NODE=$(jq -r '.nodes.root.inputs["${{ inputs.input }}"]' flake.lock)
    40→        REV=$(jq -r ".nodes[\"${NODE}\"].locked.rev" flake.lock)
    41→        echo "node=${NODE}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    42→        echo "rev=${REV}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    43→
    44→    - name: Update flake input
    45→      shell: bash
    46→      run: nix flake update "${{ inputs.input }}"
    47→
    48→    - name: Get updated revision
    49→      id: updated
    50→      shell: bash
    51→      run: |
    52→        NODE="${{ steps.current.outputs.node }}"
    53→        REV=$(jq -r ".nodes[\"${NODE}\"].locked.rev" flake.lock)
    54→        echo "rev=${REV}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    55→
    56→    - name: Check release age
    57→      if: inputs.minimum-release-age-days != '0' && steps.current.outputs.rev != steps.updated.outputs.rev
    58→      id: age-check
    59→      shell: bash
    60→      run: |
    61→        COMMIT_DATE=$(gh api "repos/${{ inputs.owner }}/${{ inputs.repo }}/commits/${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }}" --jq '.commit.committer.date')
    62→        COMMIT_TS=$(date -d "$COMMIT_DATE" +%s)
    63→        THRESHOLD_TS=$(date -d "${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }} days ago" +%s)
    64→
    65→        if [ "$COMMIT_TS" -gt "$THRESHOLD_TS" ]; then
    66→          echo "Commit ${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }} is less than ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }} days old, skipping"
    67→          git checkout -- flake.lock
    68→          echo "skip=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    69→        else
    70→          echo "skip=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    71→        fi
    72→      env:
    73→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ inputs.token }}
    74→
    75→    - name: Check for changes
    76→      id: diff
    77→      if: steps.age-check.outputs.skip != 'true'
    78→      shell: bash
    79→      run: |
    80→        if git diff --quiet flake.lock; then
    81→          echo "No changes for ${{ inputs.input }}"
    82→          echo "changed=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    83→        else
    84→          echo "changed=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    85→        fi
    86→
    87→    - name: Get llm-agents package versions
    88→      if: inputs.input == 'llm-agents' && steps.diff.outputs.changed == 'true'
    89→      id: versions
    90→      shell: bash
    91→      run: |
    92→        OLD_REV="${{ steps.current.outputs.rev }}"
    93→        NEW_REV="${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }}"
    94→        PACKAGES="claude-code ccusage codex opencode vibe-kanban"
    95→
    96→        TABLE="| Package | Old | New |"
    97→        TABLE="${TABLE}
    98→        |---|---|---|"
    99→
   100→        for pkg in $PACKAGES; do
   101→          OLD_VER=$(nix eval --raw "github:numtide/llm-agents.nix/${OLD_REV}#packages.x86_64-linux.${pkg}.version" 2>/dev/null || echo "N/A")
   102→          NEW_VER=$(nix eval --raw "github:numtide/llm-agents.nix/${NEW_REV}#packages.x86_64-linux.${pkg}.version" 2>/dev/null || echo "N/A")
   103→          TABLE="${TABLE}
   104→        | \`${pkg}\` | ${OLD_VER} | ${NEW_VER} |"
   105→        done
   106→
   107→        {
   108→          echo "table<<VERSIONS_EOF"
   109→          echo "$TABLE"
   110→          echo "VERSIONS_EOF"
   111→        } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   112→
   113→    - name: Create or update PR
   114→      if: steps.diff.outputs.changed == 'true'
   115→      shell: bash
   116→      run: |
   117→        INPUT="${{ inputs.input }}"
   118→        BRANCH="update-flake-${INPUT}"
   119→        OLD_REV="${{ steps.current.outputs.rev }}"
   120→        NEW_REV="${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }}"
   121→        SHORT_OLD="${OLD_REV:0:7}"
   122→        SHORT_NEW="${NEW_REV:0:7}"
   123→        COMPARE_URL="https://github.com/${{ inputs.owner }}/${{ inputs.repo }}/compare/${OLD_REV}...${NEW_REV}"
   124→
   125→        # Commit and push
   126→        git checkout -B "$BRANCH"
   127→        git add flake.lock
   128→        git commit -m "flake.lock: update ${INPUT}"
   129→        git push -f origin "$BRANCH"
   130→
   131→        # Build PR body
   132→        BODY="Update \`${INPUT}\` input.
   133→
   134→        **Diff**: [\`${SHORT_OLD}...${SHORT_NEW}\`](${COMPARE_URL})"
   135→
   136→        VERSION_TABLE="${{ steps.versions.outputs.table }}"
   137→        if [ -n "$VERSION_TABLE" ]; then
   138→          BODY="${BODY}
   139→
   140→        ## Package Versions
   141→
   142→        ${VERSION_TABLE}"
   143→        fi
   144→
   145→        # Create or update PR
   146→        EXISTING_PR=$(gh pr list --head "$BRANCH" --state open --json number --jq '.[0].number' 2>/dev/null || true)
   147→
   148→        if [ -n "$EXISTING_PR" ] && [ "$EXISTING_PR" != "null" ]; then
   149→          echo "Updating existing PR #${EXISTING_PR}"
   150→          gh pr edit "$EXISTING_PR" --body "$BODY"
   151→        else
   152→          echo "Creating new PR"
   153→          gh pr create \
   154→            --title "flake.lock: update ${INPUT}" \
   155→            --body "$BODY" \
   156→            --head "$BRANCH"
   157→        fi
   158→
   159→        # Enable auto-merge
   160→        PR_NUMBER=$(gh pr list --head "$BRANCH" --state open --json number --jq '.[0].number')
   161→        if [ -n "$PR_NUMBER" ] && [ "$PR_NUMBER" != "null" ]; then
   162→          gh pr merge "$PR_NUMBER" --auto --squash
   163→        fi
   164→      env:
   165→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ inputs.token }}
   166→
```

     4→inputs:

     6→    description: GitHub App ID
     7→    required: true
     8→  app-private-key:
     9→    description: GitHub App private key
    10→    required: true
    11→  app-slug:
    12→    description: GitHub App slug (URL末尾部分)
    13→    required: true
    14→
    15→outputs:
    16→  token:
    17→    description: GitHub App installation token
    18→    value: ${{ steps.token.outputs.token }}
    19→
    20→runs:
    21→  using: composite
    22→  steps:
    23→    - id: token
    24→      uses: actions/create-github-app-token@v2
    25→      with:
    26→        app-id: ${{ inputs.app-id }}
    27→        private-key: ${{ inputs.app-private-key }}
    28→
    29→    - id: bot-identity
    30→      shell: bash
    31→      run: |
    32→        BOT_USER_ID=$(gh api "/users/${{ inputs.app-slug }}%5Bbot%5D" --jq '.id')
    33→        echo "name=${{ inputs.app-slug }}[bot]" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    34→        echo "email=${BOT_USER_ID}+${{ inputs.app-slug }}[bot]@users.noreply.github.com" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    35→      env:
    36→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.token.outputs.token }}
    37→
    38→    - shell: bash
    39→      run: |
    40→        git config user.name "${{ steps.bot-identity.outputs.name }}"
    41→        git config user.email "${{ steps.bot-identity.outputs.email }}"
    42→

     1→name: Update Flake Inputs (Reusable)
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  workflow_call:
     5→    inputs:
     6→      exclude-inputs:
     7→        description: Comma-separated list of inputs to exclude
     8→        type: string
     9→        default: ""
    10→      include-inputs:
    11→        description: Comma-separated list of inputs to include (if set, only these)
    12→        type: string
    13→        default: ""
    14→      skip-delay:
    15→        description: Skip random delay between updates
    16→        type: boolean
    17→        default: false
    18→      minimum-release-age-days:
    19→        description: Minimum age in days for the release commit
    20→        type: number
    21→        default: 0
    22→    secrets:
    23→      FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_ID:
    24→        required: true
    25→      FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:
    26→        required: true
    27→
    28→jobs:
    29→  discover:
    30→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    31→    outputs:
    32→      matrix: ${{ steps.discover.outputs.matrix }}
    33→    steps:
    34→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    35→      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix
    36→      - id: discover
    37→        uses: ./.github/actions/discover-flake-inputs
    38→        with:
    39→          exclude-inputs: ${{ inputs.exclude-inputs }}
    40→          include-inputs: ${{ inputs.include-inputs }}
    41→          skip-delay: ${{ inputs.skip-delay }}
    42→
    43→  update:
    44→    needs: discover
    45→    if: ${{ fromJSON(needs.discover.outputs.matrix).include[0] != null }}
    46→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    47→    strategy:
    48→      fail-fast: false
    49→      matrix: ${{ fromJSON(needs.discover.outputs.matrix) }}
    50→    steps:
    51→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    52→
    53→      - id: setup-bot
    54→        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-git-bot
    55→        with:
    56→          app-id: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_ID }}
    57→          app-private-key: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
    58→          app-slug: ${{ vars.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_SLUG }}
    59→
    60→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    61→        with:
    62→          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
    63→
    64→      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix
    65→
    66→      - uses: ./.github/actions/update-flake-input
    67→        with:
    68→          input: ${{ matrix.input }}
    69→          owner: ${{ matrix.owner }}
    70→          repo: ${{ matrix.repo }}
    71→          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
    72→          skip-delay: ${{ matrix.skip_delay }}
    73→          minimum-release-age-days: ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }}
    74→
    75→  summary:
    76→    needs: [discover, update]
    77→    if: always()
    78→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    79→    steps:
    80→      - run: |
    81→          echo "## Update Summary" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
    82→          echo "- Discover: ${{ needs.discover.result }}" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
    83→          echo "- Update: ${{ needs.update.result }}" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
    84→

{"result":"You're asking about the automatic rebase workflow for Nix flake updates, specific Git hooks (`post-merge`, `post-rewrite`) that trigger a Nix configuration switch, and other Nix-related automation outside of the main update workflow.\n\n## Auto-rebase Workflow\n\nThe auto-rebase workflow is defined in `.github/workflows/auto-rebase.yaml` . This workflow is triggered on pushes to the `main` branch or manually via `workflow_dispatch` . Its primary purpose is to rebase open pull requests that are related to flake updates .\n\nThe workflow performs the following steps:\n1.  **Sets up Git bot identity**: It uses the `setup-git-bot` action to authenticate with GitHub .\n2.  **Checks out the repository**: It checks out the repository using the bot's token .\n3.  **Identifies flake update PRs**: It lists all open pull requests whose head branch names start with `update-flake-` .\n4.  **Rebases PRs**: For each identified PR, it fetches the branch, checks if it's behind `main`, and if so, performs a rebase onto `origin/main` . If the rebase is successful, it force pushes the branch .\n\n## Git Hooks for Automatic Nix Switch\n\nThe Git hooks for automatic Nix configuration switching are installed via Home Manager activation, as defined in `nix/modules/home/git-hooks.nix` . These hooks check for changes in Nix-related files (`flake.nix`, `flake.lock`, `nix/`, `aqua/aqua.yaml`) and automatically run `nix run .#switch` if changes are detected .\n\n### `post-merge` Hook\nThe `post-merge` hook is located in `nix/modules/home/git-hooks.nix` . It checks if Nix configuration files have changed between `HEAD@{1}` (the previous `HEAD`) and `HEAD` after a merge . If changes are found, it executes `nix run .#switch` to apply the new configuration .\n\n```bash\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Generated by nix/modules/home/git-hooks.nix\nif git diff HEAD@{1}..HEAD --name-only 2>/dev/null | grep -qE '^(flake.nix|flake.lock|nix/|aqua/aqua.yaml)'; then\n  echo \"Nix configuration changed. Applying changes...\"\n  nix run .#switch\nfi\n``` \n\n### `post-rewrite` Hook\nThe `post-rewrite` hook is also defined in `nix/modules/home/git-hooks.nix` . This hook is specifically triggered after a `git pull --rebase` operation . It only runs if the rewrite operation was a \"rebase\" (not \"amend\") . It compares `ORIG_HEAD` (before rebase) with `HEAD` (after rebase) for changes in Nix configuration files . If changes are detected, it runs `nix run .#switch` .\n\n```bash\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Generated by nix/modules/home/git-hooks.nix\n# post-rewrite receives: $1=rebase or amend\n# Only run after rebase (not amend, which is handled by post-commit)\nif [ \"$1\" != \"rebase\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compare ORIG_HEAD (before rebase) with HEAD (after rebase)\nif git diff ORIG_HEAD..HEAD --name-only 2>/dev/null | grep -qE '^(flake.nix|flake.lock|nix/|aqua/aqua.yaml)'; then\n  echo \"Nix configuration changed after rebase. Applying changes...\"\n  nix run .#switch\nfi\n``` \n\nOther related Git hooks include `pre-commit` for running `treefmt`  and `post-commit` for applying Nix changes after a commit . There is also a `post-checkout` hook that applies Nix changes after a checkout .\n\n## Other Nix Update Automation\n\nBeyond the main flake update workflows, there are other automated processes related to Nix updates:\n\n### `update-node-packages` Workflow\nThis workflow, defined in `.github/workflows/update-node-packages.yaml` , runs weekly to update npm packages managed via `buildNpmPackage` . It creates a pull request with the updated packages and enables auto-merge . This workflow uses the `update.sh` script located at `nix/packages/node/update.sh` .\n\n### `nix-build` Workflow\nThe `nix-build` workflow in `.github/workflows/nix-build.yaml`  is a CI workflow that builds the Nix configuration on pushes to `main` or pull requests . It checks for changes in Nix-related files (`flake.nix`, `flake.lock`, `nix/**`)  and builds the Home Manager configuration for Linux and Darwin . This ensures that the Nix configuration remains buildable.\n\n### `update-flake-frequent.yaml`\nThe `llm-agents` and `claude-code-overlay` flake inputs are updated every 3 hours without a minimum release age via the `_update-flake-reusable.yaml` workflow, which is called by `update-flake-frequent.yaml` . This ensures that AI tool packages are kept up-to-date . The `update-flake-input` action is used to manage these updates, including extracting package versions for PR titles .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe `nix run .#switch` command mentioned in the Git hooks is an application defined in `flake.nix` . It builds and activates the appropriate Nix configuration (nix-darwin for macOS or Home Manager for Linux) . The `dotfilesDir` variable used in `nix/modules/home/git-hooks.nix`  is passed from `nix/modules/home/default.nix`  and defaults to `~/ghq/github.com/ryoppippi/dotfiles` .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Package Overlays and Custom Packages (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#2.6)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.com/search/show-me-the-exact-content-of-t_e9991043-6735-4241-8554-0f815626c4b5\n"}
```

## 現状の比較

**既に実装済み（ryoppippi/dotfiles と同等）:**
- `_update-flake-reusable.yaml` - 再利用可能ワークフロー
- `update-flake.yaml` - 安定版更新（毎日、3日待機）
- `update-flake-frequent.yaml` - 頻繁更新（llm-agents、3時間ごと）
- `discover-flake-inputs` / `update-flake-input` / `setup-nix` / `setup-git-bot` アクション
- リリース年齢チェック、llm-agents バージョンテーブル、自動マージ

**ryoppippi にあって不足しているもの:**

| 機能 | 説明 |
|------|------|
| `auto-rebase.yaml` | main push時にupdate PRを自動rebase |
| Git hooks (Home Manager管理) | `post-merge` / `post-rewrite` でnixファイル変更時に自動 `nix run .#switch` |
| `nix-build.yaml` CI | PR/pushでnix configのビルド検証 |

{"result":"The `nix-build.yaml` workflow is a GitHub Actions workflow responsible for validating Nix builds across different architectures . It is triggered by pushes to the `main` branch, pull requests, and manual `workflow_dispatch` events . The workflow uses path filtering to optimize runs, only executing builds if Nix-related files have changed .\n\n## Workflow Content\n\nThe `nix-build.yaml` workflow is defined as follows:\n\n```yaml\nname: 'CI: Nix build'\n\non:\n  push:\n    branches:\n      - main\n  pull_request:\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}\n  cancel-in-progress: true\n\njobs:\n  # Check if Nix-related files changed\n  changes:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm\n    outputs:\n      nix: ${{ steps.filter.outputs.nix }}\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2\n      - uses: dorny/paths-filter@de90cc6fb38fc0963ad72b210f1f284cd68cea36 # v3.0.2\n        id: filter\n        with:\n          filters: |\n            nix:\n              - 'flake.nix'\n              - 'flake.lock'\n              - 'nix/**'\n              - '.github/workflows/nix-build.yaml'\n              - '.github/actions/setup-nix/**'\n\n  build-ubuntu:\n    needs: changes\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 30\n    steps:\n      - name: Skip if no Nix changes\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix != 'true' && github.event_name != 'workflow_dispatch'\n        run: echo \"No Nix-related changes detected, skipping build\"\n\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n        uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2\n\n      - name: Setup Nix\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix\n        with:\n          cachix-auth-token: ${{ secrets.CACHIX_AUTH_TOKEN }}\n\n      - name: Build Linux (Home Manager) configuration\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n        run: |\n          nix build .#homeConfigurations.ryoppippi.activationPackage \\\n            --print-build-logs \\\n            --show-trace\n\n  build-ubuntu-arm:\n    needs: changes\n    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm\n    timeout-minutes: 30\n    steps:\n      - name: Skip if no Nix changes\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix != 'true' && github.event_name != 'workflow_dispatch'\n        run: echo \"No Nix-related changes detected, skipping build\"\n\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n        uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2\n\n      - name: Setup Nix\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix\n        with:\n          cachix-auth-token: ${{ secrets.CACHIX_AUTH_TOKEN }}\n\n      - name: Build Linux (Home Manager) configuration for aarch64\n        if: needs.changes.outputs.nix == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n        run: |\n          nix build .#homeConfigurations.ryoppippi-aarch64.activationPackage \\\n            --print-build-logs \\\n            --show-trace\n```\n\n\n### Triggers\nThe workflow is triggered on:\n*   `push` events to the `main` branch .\n*   `pull_request` events .\n*   `workflow_dispatch` events, allowing manual triggering .\n\n### Concurrency\nIt uses a concurrency group to ensure only one workflow run per branch is active at a time, canceling any in-progress runs if a new one is triggered .\n\n### Jobs and Path Filtering\n\nThe workflow consists of three jobs: `changes`, `build-ubuntu`, and `build-ubuntu-arm` .\n\n#### `changes` Job\nThis job uses `dorny/paths-filter@v3` to determine if any Nix-related files have changed . The paths it monitors include `flake.nix`, `flake.lock`, any files within the `nix/` directory, the `nix-build.yaml` workflow itself, and files in the `.github/actions/setup-nix/` directory . The output `nix` indicates whether relevant changes were detected . This job runs on an `ubuntu-24.04-arm` runner .\n\n#### `build-ubuntu` Job (Linux x86_64)\nThis job is responsible for building the Linux (Home Manager) configuration for `x86_64` architecture .\n*   It depends on the `changes` job .\n*   It runs on an `ubuntu-latest` runner .\n*   It includes a step to skip the build if no Nix changes were detected by the `changes` job, unless the workflow was manually dispatched .\n*   It checks out the repository .\n*   It sets up Nix using a custom action `./.github/actions/setup-nix`, passing a `CACHIX_AUTH_TOKEN` secret for Cachix authentication .\n*   The build command is `nix build .#homeConfigurations.ryoppippi.activationPackage --print-build-logs --show-trace` .\n\n#### `build-ubuntu-arm` Job (Linux aarch64)\nThis job builds the Linux (Home Manager) configuration for `aarch64` architecture .\n*   It also depends on the `changes` job .\n*   It runs on an `ubuntu-24.04-arm` runner .\n*   Similar to `build-ubuntu`, it skips if no Nix changes are detected (unless manually dispatched) .\n*   It checks out the repository .\n*   It sets up Nix using the same custom action `./.github/actions/setup-nix` with the `CACHIX_AUTH_TOKEN` .\n*   The build command is `nix build .#homeConfigurations.ryoppippi-aarch64.activationPackage --print-build-logs --show-trace` .\n\n### Handling Linux and Darwin Builds\nThe `nix-build.yaml` workflow explicitly handles Linux builds for both `x86_64` and `aarch64` architectures through the `build-ubuntu` and `build-ubuntu-arm` jobs, respectively . Each job targets a specific Home Manager activation package: `.#homeConfigurations.ryoppippi.activationPackage` for `x86_64` and `.#homeConfigurations.ryoppippi-aarch64.activationPackage` for `aarch64`  .\n\nIt's important to note that while the `flake.nix` defines `darwinConfigurations` for macOS builds , the `nix-build.yaml` workflow itself does not directly build Darwin configurations. The `nix-diff.yaml` workflow, however, explicitly mentions that `darwinConfigurations` cannot be built on Linux runners . Therefore, the `nix-build.yaml` focuses solely on Linux builds across different architectures.\n\n## Notes\nThe `nix-build.yaml` workflow is part of a larger CI validation pipeline described in the \"Automation and CI/CD\" wiki page . The `setup-nix` action used in the build jobs is a custom composite action that installs Nix and configures Cachix binary caches for optimized build times . The `CACHIX_AUTH_TOKEN` is used to enable write access to the `ryoppippi.cachix.org` cache, ensuring successful builds are cached .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Automation and CI/CD (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#8)\n- [Cachix and Build Optimization (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#8.3)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.com/search/show-me-the-exact-content-of-t_9b58becd-9525-4e19-95d8-6afe9704a8f9\n"}

    96→  outputs =
    97→    inputs@{
    98→      self,
    99→      nixpkgs,
   100→      flake-parts,
   101→      treefmt-nix,
   102→      ghostty,
   103→      home-manager,
   104→      niri,
   105→      llm-agents,
   106→      gh-nippou,
   107→      gh-graph,

   109→      version-lsp,
   110→      agent-skills,

   113→      ui-ux-pro-max-skill,

   121→    }:
   122→    let
   123→      # Helper functions for home-manager
   124→      helpers = import ./nix/modules/lib/helpers { lib = nixpkgs.lib; };
   125→
   126→      # Dotfiles directory path helper
   127→      mkDotfilesDir = homeDir: "${homeDir}/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix";
   128→
   129→      # Local agent skills (filtered from this repo)
   130→      local-skills = nix-filter.lib {
   131→        root = self;
   132→        include = [ "agents/skills" ];
   133→      };
   134→
   135→      # Overlay for external flake packages (parameterized by system)
   136→      mkExternalOverlay =
   137→        system: final: prev:
   138→        {
   139→          claude-code = llm-agents.packages.${system}.claude-code;
   140→          ccusage = llm-agents.packages.${system}.ccusage;
   141→          codex = llm-agents.packages.${system}.codex;
   142→          opencode = llm-agents.packages.${system}.opencode;
   143→          vibe-kanban = llm-agents.packages.${system}.vibe-kanban;
   144→          version-lsp = version-lsp.packages.${system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {
   145→            doCheck = false;
   146→          });
   147→          gh-nippou = gh-nippou.packages.${system}.default;
   148→          gh-graph = gh-graph.packages.${system}.default;
   149→        }
   150→        // (
   151→          if builtins.match ".*-linux" system != null then
   152→            {
   153→              ghostty = ghostty.packages.${system}.default;
   154→            }
   155→          else
   156→            { }
   157→        );
   158→
   159→      # Custom package overlays
   160→      customOverlay = import ./nix/overlays;
   161→
   162→      # mkSystem helper function (NixOS)
   163→      mkSystem =
   164→        {
   165→          host,
   166→          system,
   167→          profile,
   168→          extraModules ? [ ],
   169→        }:
   170→        let
   171→          dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/home/yuta";

   174→          inherit system;
   175→          specialArgs = {

   177→              helpers
   178→              dotfilesDir
   179→              home-manager
   180→              niri
   181→              local-skills
   182→              anthropic-skills
   183→              vercel-skills
   184→              ui-ux-pro-max-skill
   185→              ast-grep-skill
   186→              agent-skills
   187→              ;
   188→          };
   189→          modules = [
   190→            ./nix/modules/linux
   191→            ./nix/hosts/${host}
   192→            ./nix/profiles/${profile}.nix
   193→            {
   194→              nixpkgs.overlays = [
   195→                (mkExternalOverlay system)
   196→                moonbit-overlay.overlays.default
   197→                customOverlay
   198→              ];
   199→              nixpkgs.config.allowUnfreePredicate =

   201→                builtins.elem (nixpkgs.lib.getName pkg) [

   206→                  "slack"
   207→                  "obsidian"

   212→            }
   213→            nix-hazkey.nixosModules.hazkey
   214→          ]

   216→        };
   217→
   218→      # mkDarwin helper function (macOS)
   219→      mkDarwin =

   221→          host,
   222→          system,
   223→          extraModules ? [ ],
   224→        }:
   225→        let
   226→          dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/Users/yuta";
   227→        in
   228→        nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem {
   229→          inherit system;
   230→          specialArgs = {

   233→              dotfilesDir

   235→              local-skills
   236→              anthropic-skills
   237→              vercel-skills

   242→              ;
   243→          };
   244→          modules = [
   245→            ./nix/modules/darwin
   246→            ./nix/hosts/${host}
   247→            ./nix/profiles/darwin.nix
   248→            {
   249→              nixpkgs.overlays = [
   250→                (mkExternalOverlay system)
   251→                moonbit-overlay.overlays.default
   252→                customOverlay
   253→                brew-nix.overlays.default
   254→              ];
   255→              nixpkgs.config.allowUnfree = true;

   257→          ]

   260→    in
   261→    flake-parts.lib.mkFlake { inherit inputs; } {
   262→      systems = [
   263→        "x86_64-linux"
   264→        "aarch64-darwin"
   265→      ];
   266→
   267→      imports = [
   268→        treefmt-nix.flakeModule
   269→        inputs.git-hooks.flakeModule
   270→      ];
   271→
   272→      perSystem =
   273→        {
   274→          pkgs,
   275→          system,
   276→          config,
   277→          ...
   278→        }:
   279→        let
   280→          isDarwin = builtins.match ".*-darwin" system != null;
   281→          hostname = if isDarwin then "darwin" else "nixos";
   282→          nom = "${pkgs.nix-output-monitor}/bin/nom";
   283→        in
   284→        {
   285→          apps = {
   286→            build = {
   287→              type = "app";
   288→              program = toString (
   289→                pkgs.writeShellScript "build" ''
   290→                  set -e
   291→                  echo "Building ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   292→                  ${nom} build ${
   293→                    if isDarwin then
   294→                      ".#darwinConfigurations.${hostname}.system"
   295→                    else
   296→                      ".#nixosConfigurations.${hostname}.config.system.build.toplevel"
   297→                  }
   298→                  echo "Build successful! Run 'nix run .#switch' to apply."
   299→                ''
   300→              );
   301→            };
   302→            switch = {
   303→              type = "app";
   304→              program = toString (
   305→                pkgs.writeShellScript "switch" ''
   306→                  set -eo pipefail
   307→                  echo "Switching to ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   308→                  ${
   309→                    if isDarwin then
   310→                      "darwin-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   311→                    else
   312→                      "sudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   313→                  }
   314→                  echo "Done!"
   315→                ''
   316→              );
   317→            };
   318→            fmt = {
   319→              type = "app";
   320→              program = toString (
   321→                pkgs.writeShellScript "treefmt-wrapper" ''
   322→                  exec ${config.treefmt.build.wrapper}/bin/treefmt "$@"
   323→                ''
   324→              );
   325→            };
   326→          };
   327→
   328→          treefmt = {
   329→            projectRootFile = "flake.nix";
   330→            programs = {
   331→              nixfmt.enable = true;
   332→              stylua.enable = true;
   333→              shfmt.enable = true;
   334→            };
   335→            settings.global.excludes = [
   336→              ".git/**"
   337→              "*.lock"
   338→            ];
   339→          };
   340→
   341→          pre-commit = {
   342→            check.enable = false;
   343→            settings.hooks = {
   344→              treefmt = {
   345→                enable = true;
   346→                package = config.treefmt.build.wrapper;
   347→              };
   348→              git-secrets = {
   349→                enable = true;
   350→                entry = "${pkgs.git-secrets}/bin/git-secrets --pre_commit_hook";
   351→                language = "system";
   352→                stages = [ "pre-commit" ];
   353→                excludes = [ "^\.direnv/" ];
   354→              };
   355→            };
   356→          };
   357→
   358→          devShells.default = pkgs.mkShell {
   359→            shellHook = ''
   360→              ${config.pre-commit.installationScript}
   361→            '';
   362→          };
   363→        };
   364→
   365→      flake = {
   366→        nixosConfigurations = {
   367→          nixos = mkSystem {
   368→            host = "nixos";
   369→            system = "x86_64-linux";
   370→            profile = "gui";
   371→            extraModules = [ ];
   372→          };
   373→        };
   374→
   375→        darwinConfigurations = {
   376→          darwin = mkDarwin {
   377→            host = "darwin";
   378→            system = "aarch64-darwin";
   379→          };
   380→        };
   381→      };
   382→    };
   383→}
   384→

Links: [{"title":"Streamline your GitHub Actions dependencies using Nix","url":"https://determinate.systems/blog/nix-github-actions/"},{"title":"Install Nix · Actions · GitHub Marketplace · GitHub","url":"https://github.com/marketplace/actions/install-nix"},{"title":"Continuous integration with GitHub Actions — nix.dev documentation","url":"https://nix.dev/guides/recipes/continuous-integration-github-actions.html"},{"title":"GitHub - DeterminateSystems/nix-installer-action: The Github Action for the Determinate Nix Installer","url":"https://github.com/DeterminateSystems/nix-installer-action"},{"title":"Building container images using nix and github actions | by Seán Murphy | Medium","url":"https://seanrmurphy.medium.com/building-container-images-using-nix-and-github-actions-ba548ab9080d"},{"title":"Faster Nix builds with GitHub Actions and actuated","url":"https://actuated.com/blog/faster-nix-builds"},{"title":"Nothing but Nix","url":"https://wimpysworld.com/posts/nothing-but-nix-github-actions/"},{"title":"Actions · NixOS/.github","url":"https://github.com/NixOS/.github/actions"},{"title":"GitHub - nix-community/nix-github-actions: A library to turn Nix Flake attribute sets into Github Actions matrices [maintainer=@adisbladis]","url":"https://github.com/nix-community/nix-github-actions"},{"title":"Deploying with GitHub Actions and more Nix","url":"https://thewagner.net/blog/2020/12/06/deploying-with-github-actions-and-more-nix/"}]

Here are the key findings from the search on **NixOS GitHub Actions runner CI builds** (with relevance to 2025/2026):

---

### Key Tools & Actions

1. **`cachix/install-nix-action`** — Installs Nix on GitHub Actions runners for Linux and macOS. You can pair it with a binary cache from `cachix-action` to speed up re-builds and share binaries across your team. The current version is **v31**.

2. **`DeterminateSystems/nix-installer-action`** — This Action installs Determinate Nix by default. You can, however, use it to install upstream Nix until January 1, 2026. This option will be available until January 1, 2026, at which point installing upstream Nix using this Action will no longer be possible.

3. **`nix-community/nix-github-actions`** — Nix-github-actions is not an action in itself but a series of templates and a Nix library to build your own CI.

---

### Disk Space Challenge & Solution

Have you ever tried to build a complex NixOS configuration in GitHub Actions only to be greeted by the dreaded "no space left on device" error? Standard GitHub Actions runners come with a paltry ~20GB of free disk space. Which sounds decent until you try to build sophisticated NixOS or Home Manager configurations. A comprehensive workstation or home lab server setup can consume 10-15GB due to all the included packages and dependencies, leaving precious little headroom for anything else.

**Nothing but Nix** (`wimpysworld/nothing-but-nix`) addresses this: Instead of ~20GB for your Nix store, you get 65GB to 130GB of sweet, glorious Nix-dedicated space.

---

### Caching with Cachix

One benefit of Nix is that CI can build and cache developer environments for every project on every branch using binary caches. Using Cachix you'll never have to waste time building a derivation twice, and you'll share built derivations with all your developers. After each job, just-built derivations are pushed to your binary cache. Before each job, derivations to be built are first substituted (if they exist) from your binary cache.

---

### Replacing Third-Party Actions with Nix

Excluding the ubiquitous checkout Action, the "no Nix" pipeline uses five different third-party Actions while the Nix pipeline only uses two (one to set up Nix and one to set up caching for Rust). The Nix-based pipeline runs faster in this repo. You do pay an up-front cost for building the shell environment, but that environment is then cached under `/nix/store` on the runner and running executables in that environment is quite "cheap" after the first run.

---

### Self-Hosted NixOS Runners

Although NixOS is not listed in the set of supported architectures and operating systems for GitHub runners, getting a GitHub runner working on NixOS is very straightforward. You can configure a self-hosted runner declaratively in your NixOS configuration using the `services.github-runners` module.

---

### Build Performance

Building Nix packages or entire NixOS systems on GitHub Actions can be slow especially if you need to build for Arm. Bringing your own metal to GitHub actions can speed up your builds. For example, building a project takes around 4 minutes and 20 seconds on the standard GitHub hosted runner, but after switching to a more powerful Actuated runner with 4 CPUs and 8GB of RAM the build time dropped to 2 minutes and 15 seconds.

---

### Important 2026 Deadline

A notable upcoming change: the **DeterminateSystems nix-installer-action** will no longer support installing upstream Nix after January 1, 2026, only Determinate Nix will be available through that action going forward. If you rely on upstream Nix, you may want to switch to `cachix/install-nix-action` or another installer before that date.

Here's a comprehensive summary of the search results covering the various Nix CI solutions for GitHub:

---

## Nix CI Solutions for GitHub: Overview

### 1. **GitHub Actions with Nix (Standard Approach)**

The `cachix/install-nix-action` installs Nix on GitHub Actions runners for Linux and macOS. You can pair it with a binary cache from `cachix-action` to speed up re-builds and share binaries across your team.

When using Nix you do pay an up-front cost for building the shell environment, but that environment is then cached under `/nix/store` on the runner and running executables in that environment is quite "cheap" after the first run.

### 2. **Garnix**

One user reports: "I have been using GitHub Actions and garnix for my needs. I like GitHub Actions because it's free. It is suitable for most of my needs, but there are some things that garnix does so well. I have found Garnix to be much smarter at reusing caches to make builds faster."

Garnix has a free tier that comes with 1,500 CI minutes per month, which has been enough for many users.

Garnix is very easy to setup because it's built with Nix in mind and comes with a built-in cache. With GitHub Actions, you have to set up the cache separately with something like Cachix or a self-hosted one with Attic. Cachix is only free if you don't store more than 5 GB.

Garnix's machines are reportedly more powerful than GitHub's — one user reports garnix could build Chromium in 1h 57m while the GitHub runner was killed after six hours.

Garnix has a global cache, meaning that for public repos, if anyone built a particular commit, it'll likely be cached.

Garnix in the non-enterprise plan only supports flakes.

#### Garnix Benchmarks

The Nix CI benchmark was created by people at garnix, and they acknowledge this may influence the results in ways favorable to garnix, including publication bias.

### 3. **Hercules CI**

Some users note that their projects use GitHub Actions + Cachix, and could benefit from the better ergonomics of Hercules CI. But having to host your own runner has been a barrier.

For complex projects using Nix as their build system (non-standard targets, mixed architectures, Import-From-Derivation), any CI system must use and fill a custom Nix cache.

### 4. **Namespace.so (Namespace Runners)**

Namespace Runners support using custom containers for GitHub Jobs. If you configure a runner profile using the web UI and use the `namespace-profile-name` runner label, you can enable caching. Cache volumes also enable caching for built-in software (Git, containerd, toolchain).

Any GitHub Actions job belonging to git branches not in the allow-list will be able to access Cache Volumes, but their changes to the cache content will not be persisted. You can also select which jobs may write to the cache, allowing you to share a cache amongst multiple jobs while some are not allowed to update the cache contents.

Although multi-platform builds are supported, only builds of the same platform as the runner itself will experience native performance. Namespace Remote Builders are configured to offer great performance for many concurrent builds.

### 5. **Self-Hosted NixOS Runners**

Although NixOS is not listed in the set of supported architectures and operating systems for GitHub runners, getting a GitHub runner working on NixOS is very straightforward.

There are several community tools to help:

- **`github-nix-ci`** (juspay): A simple NixOS & nix-darwin module for self-hosting GitHub runners on your machines (which could be a remote server or your personal MacBook), providing self-hosted CI for both personal and organization-wide repositories on GitHub.

- **SrvOS** (nix-community): This module looks at how to install a GitHub runner in your own NixOS infrastructure, making sure the environment is scalable and secure. It installs one or more self-hosted GitHub action runners, along with a Cachix watch store service with the most secure defaults.

### Key Considerations

| Feature | GitHub Actions + Nix | Garnix | Hercules CI | Namespace.so |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Hosting** | GitHub-hosted or self-hosted | Managed | Self-hosted agent | Managed |
| **Cache** | Cachix / Attic / magic-nix-cache | Built-in global cache | Custom Nix cache | Cache Volumes |
| **Flakes** | Optional | Required (non-enterprise) | Optional | N/A (general runner) |
| **Free Tier** | Yes (GitHub free mins) | 1,500 mins/month | No (self-hosted) | Varies |
| **NixOS Tests** | Requires KVM | Supported | Supported | Depends on config |

Determinate Systems argues that Actions are a problematic and often superfluous abstraction and that you should consider using Nix to make your pipelines dramatically more reproducible and ergonomically sound.

# NixOS CI Approaches Described

The post outlines **two primary CI strategies** for NixOS configurations:

## 1. Garnix (Recommended)

The author strongly prefers **Garnix**, a Nix-native CI service offering a free tier with "1,500 CI minutes per month." Key advantages highlighted:

- Built specifically for Nix with integrated caching
- Superior performance (e.g., building an Android kernel in 14 minutes vs. 45+ minutes on GitHub Actions)
- Minimal setup required—just add a `garnix.yaml` configuration file
- Shared public cache at `https://cache.garnix.io`

The default `garnix.yaml` automatically builds packages, dev shells, and NixOS/Darwin/Home configurations for x86_64-linux.

## 2. GitHub Actions (Alternative)

For those preferring GitHub Actions, the post demonstrates a complete workflow using:

- **Cachix** for external caching (free tier: 5GB limit)
- Matrix strategy to build multiple machines in parallel
- The `cachix/cachix-action` to integrate cache uploads
- Build command: `nix build -L .#nixosConfigurations.${{ matrix.machine }}.config.system.build.toplevel`

The author notes GitHub Actions runners lack sufficient memory for demanding builds, making Garnix preferable despite GitHub Actions being "free."

Neither approach uses self-hosted runners; both leverage cloud infrastructure with external caching.
```

> AGENT

良い質問です。まず前提を整理します。

## NixOS 設定のビルドに NixOS は不要

`nix build .#nixosConfigurations.nixos.config.system.build.toplevel` は **Ubuntu + Nix** でも動きます。ryoppippi を含め、ほとんどの人はこの方法です。NixOS runner 自体は必須ではありません。

## みんなのアプローチ

| 方式 | 概要 | コスト |
|------|------|--------|
| **GitHub Actions + Nix on Ubuntu** | `cachix/install-nix-action` で Nix をインストール。ryoppippi はこれ | 無料 (GitHub free分) |
| **[Garnix](https://garnix.io)** | Nix ネイティブ CI。`garnix.yaml` 置くだけ。キャッシュ内蔵で高速 | 無料枠 1,500分/月 |
| **Self-hosted NixOS runner** | 自前の NixOS マシンで `services.github-runners` を設定 | 自前サーバー代 |
| **[Namespace.so](https://namespace.so)** | マネージド runner。カスタムコンテナ対応 | 有料 |

## 各方式の特徴

**GitHub Actions + Nix (標準)** - 今の `setup-nix` action でそのまま動く。ディスク容量が ~20GB と少ないのが難点。[nothing-but-nix](https://wimpysworld.com/posts/nothing-but-nix-github-actions/) で 65-130GB に拡張できる。

**[Garnix](https://cy7.sh/posts/ci-nixos/)** - 設定が最も簡単。`garnix.yaml` を置くだけで `nixosConfigurations` を自動ビルド。グローバルキャッシュで高速。GitHub Actions より[パフォーマンスが良い](https://garnix-io.github.io/benchmarks/)という報告多数。

**Self-hosted** - NixOS マシンがあるなら `services.github-runners` で宣言的に runner を設定可能。[juspay/github-nix-ci](https://github.com/juspay/github-nix-ci) や [SrvOS](https://nix-community.github.io/srvos/github_actions_runner/) が便利。

## おすすめ

「NixOS でビルドしたい」の意図次第ですが:

- **Nix でビルドできればOK** → 今の `setup-nix` action + Ubuntu runner で十分。最小限の追加で済む
- **Nix ネイティブな CI が欲しい** → Garnix が最も手軽（YAML 1ファイル追加）
- **本当に NixOS runner が欲しい** → self-hosted runner を NixOS の設定に組み込む

{"result":"The `auto-rebase.yaml` workflow is responsible for automatically rebasing pull requests that update Nix flakes. It is triggered on pushes to the `main` branch or manually via `workflow_dispatch` . The workflow's full YAML content includes steps for setting up a Git bot, checking out the repository, finding relevant PRs, determining if they are behind `main`, and then performing the rebase and force push .\n\n## `auto-rebase.yaml` Workflow Content\n\nThe complete YAML content of the `auto-rebase.yaml` workflow is as follows:\n\n\n### Triggers\n\nThe workflow is triggered by two events:\n*   `push` events to the `main` branch .\n*   `workflow_dispatch` events, allowing manual triggering .\n\n### Concurrency\n\nThe workflow uses a concurrency group named `auto-rebase` and is configured to not cancel in-progress runs (`cancel-in-progress: false`) . This prevents race conditions during rebasing operations .\n\n### Jobs\n\nThe workflow contains a single job named `rebase-prs` .\n\n#### Job Conditions and Runner\n\nThe `rebase-prs` job runs on an `ubuntu-slim` runner . It executes only if the event is a `workflow_dispatch` or if the `head_commit.committer.name` is 'GitHub', which typically indicates a merge commit from an auto-merge action .\n\n#### Steps\n\n1.  **Checkout repository (for local actions)**: This step checks out the repository to make local actions available .\n2.  **Set up git bot identity**: This step uses the `setup-git-bot` action to configure a bot identity using GitHub App credentials . The `app-id` and `private-key` are retrieved from GitHub secrets . The output `token` from this step is used for subsequent authenticated Git operations .\n3.  **Checkout repository (with bot token)**: The repository is checked out again, this time using the bot's token for authentication and with `fetch-depth: 0` to ensure the full history is available for rebase operations .\n4.  **Rebase open flake update PRs**: This is the core step that performs the rebase logic .\n    *   It first finds all open pull requests whose head branch names start with `update-flake-` using the `gh pr list` command .\n    *   If no such PRs are found, the step exits .\n    *   For each found PR, it fetches the branch .\n    *   It then checks if the branch is behind `main` using `git rev-list --count` .\n    *   If the branch is behind `main`, it checks out the branch, performs a `git rebase origin/main`, and if successful, force pushes the rebased branch .\n    *   If the rebase fails, it aborts the rebase .\n    *   Finally, it checks out `main` again .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe `auto-rebase.yaml` workflow is a critical component of the repository's auto-maintenance cycle, ensuring that automated update PRs remain up-to-date with the `main` branch . This workflow works in conjunction with other automated workflows, such as those that create `update-flake-*` PRs, which often have auto-merge enabled . The `ubuntu-slim` runner is chosen for this workflow due to its minimal requirements for Git operations .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Automation and CI/CD (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#8)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/.github/workflows/auto-rebase.yaml",
  "content": "name: \"Auto-rebase update PRs\"\n\non:\n  push:\n    branches:\n      - main\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: auto-rebase\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\njobs:\n  rebase-prs:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm\n    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' || github.event.head_commit.committer.name == 'GitHub'\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - id: setup-bot\n        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-git-bot\n        with:\n          app-id: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_ID }}\n          app-private-key: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}\n          app-slug: ${{ vars.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_SLUG }}\n\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Rebase open flake update PRs\n        shell: bash\n        run: |\n          BRANCHES=$(gh pr list --state open --json headRefName --jq '.[].headRefName | select(startswith(\"update-flake-\"))')\n\n          if [ -z \"$BRANCHES\" ]; then\n            echo \"No update-flake PRs found\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          for BRANCH in $BRANCHES; do\n            echo \"--- Processing $BRANCH ---\"\n            git fetch origin \"$BRANCH\"\n\n            BEHIND=$(git rev-list --count \"origin/$BRANCH..origin/main\")\n            if [ \"$BEHIND\" -eq 0 ]; then\n              echo \"$BRANCH is up to date\"\n              continue\n            fi\n\n            echo \"$BRANCH is $BEHIND commits behind main, rebasing...\"\n            git checkout \"$BRANCH\"\n\n            if git rebase origin/main; then\n              git push --force-with-lease origin \"$BRANCH\"\n              echo \"$BRANCH rebased successfully\"\n            else\n              echo \"$BRANCH rebase failed, aborting\"\n              git rebase --abort\n            fi\n\n            git checkout main\n          done\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

作成しました。ポイント:

- **トリガー**: main への push（auto-merge 由来のみ）+ 手動実行
- **concurrency**: `cancel-in-progress: false` で rebase 中の別実行を防止
- **対象**: `update-flake-*` ブランチの open PR のみ
- **安全策**: `--force-with-lease`（通常の `--force` より安全）、rebase 失敗時は `--abort` でスキップ
- **認証**: 既存の `setup-git-bot` action を再利用

これで ryoppippi/dotfiles と比較して残っているのは **Git hooks (Home Manager管理)** のみです。`post-merge` / `post-rewrite` で nix ファイル変更時に自動 `nix run .#switch` する機能です。必要であれば続けて実装できます。

name: 'Bot: Auto-rebase PRs'

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  workflow_dispatch:

concurrency:
  group: auto-rebase
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  rebase-prs:
    runs-on: ubuntu-slim
    # Run on: 1) merge commits (auto-merge), or 2) manual trigger
    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' || github.event.head_commit.committer.name == 'GitHub'
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository (for local actions)
        uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2

      - name: Set up git bot identity
        id: bot
        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-git-bot
        with:
          app-id: ${{ secrets.RYOPPIPPI_NIX_UPDATER_APP_ID }}
          private-key: ${{ secrets.RYOPPIPPI_NIX_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}

      - name: Checkout repository (with bot token)
        uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
        with:
          token: ${{ steps.bot.outputs.token }}
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: Rebase open flake update PRs
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.bot.outputs.token }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail

          echo "=== Finding open flake update PRs ==="

          # Get all open PRs with update-flake- branches
          prs=$(gh pr list --state open --json number,headRefName --jq '.[] | select(.headRefName | startswith("update-flake-")) | "\(.number) \(.headRefName)"')

          if [ -z "$prs" ]; then
            echo "No open flake update PRs found"
            exit 0
          fi

          echo "Found PRs to rebase:"
          echo "$prs"

          # Process each PR
          echo "$prs" | while read -r pr_number branch_name; do
            echo ""
            echo "=== Processing PR #$pr_number (branch: $branch_name) ==="

            # Fetch the branch
            git fetch origin "$branch_name"

            # Check if branch is behind main
            behind_count=$(git rev-list --count "origin/$branch_name..origin/main")

            if [ "$behind_count" -eq 0 ]; then
              echo "Branch is up to date, skipping"
              continue
            fi

            echo "Branch is $behind_count commits behind main, rebasing..."

            # Checkout and rebase
            git checkout -B "$branch_name" "origin/$branch_name"

            if git rebase origin/main; then
              echo "Rebase successful, force pushing..."
              git push --force origin "$branch_name"
              echo "✅ PR #$pr_number rebased successfully"
            else
              echo "⚠️ Rebase failed for PR #$pr_number, aborting"
              git rebase --abort
            fi

            # Return to main
            git checkout main
          done

          echo ""
          echo "=== Rebase complete ==="
```

< name: 'Discover Flake Inputs'

<             if [ "$input" = "$exclude_input" ]; then
<               return 0
<             fi
<           done
<           return 1
<         }
< 
<         # Function to check if input should skip delay
<         should_skip_delay() {
<           local input="$1"
<           for skip_input in "${skip_delay_array[@]}"; do
<             if [ "$input" = "$skip_input" ]; then
<               return 0
<             fi
<           done
<           return 1
<         }
< 
<         # Initialise array for matrix items
<         declare -a matrix_items=()
< 
<         echo "Discovering flake inputs..."
< 
<         if [ ! -f "flake.lock" ]; then
<           echo "No flake.lock found"
<           echo "matrix={\"include\":[]}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
<           echo "has-updates=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
<           exit 0
<         fi
< 
<         # Get flake metadata
<         if ! metadata=$(nix flake metadata --json --no-write-lock-file 2>/dev/null); then
<           echo "Failed to get flake metadata"
<           exit 1
<         fi
< 
<         if [ -n "$INPUTS" ]; then
<           requested_inputs="$INPUTS"
<         else
<           # Get all direct inputs (not transitive dependencies)
<           requested_inputs=$(echo "$metadata" | jq -r '.locks.nodes.root.inputs | keys[]' | sort)
<         fi
< 
<         for input in $requested_inputs; do
<           # Check if this input should be excluded
<           if [ ${#exclude_array[@]} -gt 0 ] && should_exclude "$input"; then
<             echo "  - $input (excluded)"
<             continue
<           fi
< 
<           # Check if this input exists in the flake
<           if ! echo "$metadata" | jq -e ".locks.nodes.\"$input\"" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
<             echo "Warning: Input $input not found, skipping"
<             continue
<           fi
< 
<           current_rev=$(echo "$metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$input\".locked.rev // \"unknown\"" | head -c 8)
<           owner=$(echo "$metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$input\".original.owner // \"unknown\"")
<           repo=$(echo "$metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$input\".original.repo // \"unknown\"")
<           ref=$(echo "$metadata" | jq -r ".locks.nodes.\"$input\".original.ref // \"\"")
< 
<           # Determine if this input should skip delay
<           if should_skip_delay "$input"; then
<             skip_delay="true"
<           else
<             skip_delay="false"
<           fi
< 
<           matrix_items+=("{\"name\":\"$input\",\"current_version\":\"$current_rev\",\"skip_delay\":$skip_delay,\"owner\":\"$owner\",\"repo\":\"$repo\",\"ref\":\"$ref\"}")
<           if [ -n "$ref" ]; then
<             echo "  - $input ($current_rev) [skip_delay=$skip_delay, ref=$ref]"
<           else
<             echo "  - $input ($current_rev) [skip_delay=$skip_delay]"
<           fi
<         done
< 
<         echo
<         echo "=== Discovery Results ==="
< 
<         # Create matrix JSON
<         if [ ${#matrix_items[@]} -eq 0 ]; then
<           matrix='{"include":[]}'
<           has_updates="false"
<           echo "No items to update"
<         else
<           matrix_json='{"include":['
<           for i in "${!matrix_items[@]}"; do
<             if [ "$i" -gt 0 ]; then
<               matrix_json+=","
<             fi
<             matrix_json+="${matrix_items[$i]}"
<           done
<           matrix_json+="]}"
< 
<           matrix="$matrix_json"
<           has_updates="true"
<           echo "Found ${#matrix_items[@]} input(s) to check"
<         fi
< 
<         echo "matrix=$matrix" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
<         echo "has-updates=$has_updates" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

>   app-id:
>     description: GitHub App ID
>     required: true
>   app-private-key:
>     description: GitHub App private key
>     required: true
>   app-slug:
>     description: GitHub App slug (URL末尾部分)
>     required: true
> 
> outputs:
>   token:
>     description: GitHub App installation token
>     value: ${{ steps.token.outputs.token }}

> runs:
>   using: composite

>       uses: actions/create-github-app-token@v2
>       with:

>         private-key: ${{ inputs.app-private-key }}
> 
>     - id: bot-identity
>       shell: bash
>       run: |
>         BOT_USER_ID=$(gh api "/users/${{ inputs.app-slug }}%5Bbot%5D" --jq '.id')
>         echo "name=${{ inputs.app-slug }}[bot]" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
>         echo "email=${BOT_USER_ID}+${{ inputs.app-slug }}[bot]@users.noreply.github.com" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
>       env:
>         GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.token.outputs.token }}
> 
>     - shell: bash
>       run: |
>         git config user.name "${{ steps.bot-identity.outputs.name }}"
>         git config user.email "${{ steps.bot-identity.outputs.email }}"

> AGENT

差分を整理します。意味のある違いだけまとめます。

## 既存ファイルの差分まとめ

### `_update-flake-reusable.yaml`

| 項目 | ryoppippi | 自分 |
|------|-----------|------|
| workflow_dispatch 用パラメータ | `inputs`, `skip-delay-inputs`, `pr-labels`, `auto-merge`, `summary-title` | なし（シンプル） |
| summary ジョブ | 設定値やステータスを詳細出力 | 最小限の2行 |
| permissions 明示 | `contents: write`, `pull-requests: write` | なし |
| action ピン | SHA ハッシュ (`@de0fac2e...`) | タグ (`@v4`) |

### `update-flake.yaml` / `update-flake-frequent.yaml`

| 項目 | ryoppippi | 自分 |
|------|-----------|------|
| 手動実行時のパラメータ | inputs, minimum-release-age-days, skip-delay を指定可能 | なし |
| frequent 対象 | `llm-agents` + `claude-code-overlay` | `llm-agents` のみ |

### `discover-flake-inputs`

| 項目 | ryoppippi | 自分 |
|------|-----------|------|
| 実装 | bash ~110行（ループ + 関数） | jq ワンライナー ~25行 |
| エラーハンドリング | `flake.lock` 欠落、metadata 取得失敗をチェック | なし |
| `has-updates` output | あり（update ジョブの条件に使用） | なし（`matrix.include[0] != null` で代替） |
| per-input skip-delay | `skip-delay-inputs` で個別指定 | グローバル `skip-delay` のみ |

### `update-flake-input`

| 項目 | ryoppippi | 自分 |
|------|-----------|------|
| バージョンチェック対象 | claude-code, codex, amp, cursor-agent, gemini-cli, opencode, goose-cli | claude-code, ccusage, codex, opencode, vibe-kanban |
| PR タイトル | バージョン差分入り (`update llm-agents (claude-code=1.0→1.1)`) | 固定 (`flake.lock: update <input>`) |
| PR ラベル | `dependencies,automated` | なし |
| outputs | `updated`, `new-version`, `pr-url` | なし |
| `ref` サポート | あり（branch/tag 指定可能） | なし |

### `setup-nix`

| 項目 | ryoppippi | 自分 |
|------|-----------|------|
| Cachix | `numtide`(読取) + `ryoppippi`(書込+authToken) | `numtide`(読取のみ, skipPush) |
| `github_access_token` | nix installer に渡している | なし |

### `setup-git-bot`
ryoppippi は 404（非公開 or 別構造）。自分の方は `app-slug` ベースの実装。

---

## 取り込む価値がありそうなもの

1. **PR タイトルにバージョン差分** - どのパッケージが更新されたか一目でわかる
2. **バージョンチェック対象の更新** - amp, gemini-cli, goose-cli の追加
3. **workflow_dispatch のパラメータ化** - 手動実行時に柔軟に指定可能
4. **action ピンを SHA に** - サプライチェーン攻撃対策
5. **PR ラベル** - フィルタリングに便利

どれか取り込みますか？

> DEVELOPER

取り込んだ良さそうなのをやって,Cachixの書き込みもしたいね,github_access_tokenについてなんの話陏婁

> AGENT

まず `github_access_token` について。

**`github_access_token`** は `cachix/install-nix-action` のパラメータで、Nix が GitHub からflake inputsを取得するときの API レート制限回避用です。匿名だと 60 req/hour しかないところ、`GITHUB_TOKEN` を渡すと 1,000 req/hour になります。CI で多数の flake input を持つ場合に効きます。

では改善を一括で実装します。

id: toolu_01HdfrG54JFYwTt1H2L59vsG
```
     1→name: Update Flake Input
     2→description: Update a single flake input, create PR, and enable auto-merge
     3→
     4→inputs:
     5→  input:
     6→    description: Flake input name
     7→    required: true
     8→  owner:
     9→    description: GitHub repository owner
    10→    required: true
    11→  repo:
    12→    description: GitHub repository name
    13→    required: true
    14→  token:
    15→    description: GitHub token for PR operations
    16→    required: true
    17→  skip-delay:
    18→    description: Skip random delay
    19→    default: "false"
    20→  minimum-release-age-days:
    21→    description: Minimum age in days for the release commit
    22→    default: "0"
    23→
    24→runs:
    25→  using: composite
    26→  steps:
    27→    - name: Random delay
    28→      if: inputs.skip-delay != 'true'
    29→      shell: bash
    30→      run: |
    31→        DELAY=$((RANDOM % 60))
    32→        echo "Sleeping ${DELAY}s to avoid rate limits"
    33→        sleep "$DELAY"
    34→
    35→    - name: Get current revision
    36→      id: current
    37→      shell: bash
    38→      run: |
    39→        NODE=$(jq -r '.nodes.root.inputs["${{ inputs.input }}"]' flake.lock)
    40→        REV=$(jq -r ".nodes[\"${NODE}\"].locked.rev" flake.lock)
    41→        echo "node=${NODE}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    42→        echo "rev=${REV}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    43→
    44→    - name: Update flake input
    45→      shell: bash
    46→      run: nix flake update "${{ inputs.input }}"
    47→
    48→    - name: Get updated revision
    49→      id: updated
    50→      shell: bash
    51→      run: |
    52→        NODE="${{ steps.current.outputs.node }}"
    53→        REV=$(jq -r ".nodes[\"${NODE}\"].locked.rev" flake.lock)
    54→        echo "rev=${REV}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    55→
    56→    - name: Check release age
    57→      if: inputs.minimum-release-age-days != '0' && steps.current.outputs.rev != steps.updated.outputs.rev
    58→      id: age-check
    59→      shell: bash
    60→      run: |
    61→        COMMIT_DATE=$(gh api "repos/${{ inputs.owner }}/${{ inputs.repo }}/commits/${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }}" --jq '.commit.committer.date')
    62→        COMMIT_TS=$(date -d "$COMMIT_DATE" +%s)
    63→        THRESHOLD_TS=$(date -d "${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }} days ago" +%s)
    64→
    65→        if [ "$COMMIT_TS" -gt "$THRESHOLD_TS" ]; then
    66→          echo "Commit ${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }} is less than ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }} days old, skipping"
    67→          git checkout -- flake.lock
    68→          echo "skip=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    69→        else
    70→          echo "skip=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    71→        fi
    72→      env:
    73→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ inputs.token }}
    74→
    75→    - name: Check for changes
    76→      id: diff
    77→      if: steps.age-check.outputs.skip != 'true'
    78→      shell: bash
    79→      run: |
    80→        if git diff --quiet flake.lock; then
    81→          echo "No changes for ${{ inputs.input }}"
    82→          echo "changed=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    83→        else
    84→          echo "changed=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    85→        fi
    86→
    87→    - name: Get llm-agents package versions
    88→      if: inputs.input == 'llm-agents' && steps.diff.outputs.changed == 'true'
    89→      id: versions
    90→      shell: bash
    91→      run: |
    92→        OLD_REV="${{ steps.current.outputs.rev }}"
    93→        NEW_REV="${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }}"
    94→        PACKAGES="claude-code ccusage codex opencode vibe-kanban"
    95→
    96→        TABLE="| Package | Old | New |"
    97→        TABLE="${TABLE}
    98→        |---|---|---|"
    99→
   100→        for pkg in $PACKAGES; do
   101→          OLD_VER=$(nix eval --raw "github:numtide/llm-agents.nix/${OLD_REV}#packages.x86_64-linux.${pkg}.version" 2>/dev/null || echo "N/A")
   102→          NEW_VER=$(nix eval --raw "github:numtide/llm-agents.nix/${NEW_REV}#packages.x86_64-linux.${pkg}.version" 2>/dev/null || echo "N/A")
   103→          TABLE="${TABLE}
   104→        | \`${pkg}\` | ${OLD_VER} | ${NEW_VER} |"
   105→        done
   106→
   107→        {
   108→          echo "table<<VERSIONS_EOF"
   109→          echo "$TABLE"
   110→          echo "VERSIONS_EOF"
   111→        } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
   112→
   113→    - name: Create or update PR
   114→      if: steps.diff.outputs.changed == 'true'
   115→      shell: bash
   116→      run: |
   117→        INPUT="${{ inputs.input }}"
   118→        BRANCH="update-flake-${INPUT}"
   119→        OLD_REV="${{ steps.current.outputs.rev }}"
   120→        NEW_REV="${{ steps.updated.outputs.rev }}"
   121→        SHORT_OLD="${OLD_REV:0:7}"
   122→        SHORT_NEW="${NEW_REV:0:7}"
   123→        COMPARE_URL="https://github.com/${{ inputs.owner }}/${{ inputs.repo }}/compare/${OLD_REV}...${NEW_REV}"
   124→
   125→        # Commit and push
   126→        git checkout -B "$BRANCH"
   127→        git add flake.lock
   128→        git commit -m "flake.lock: update ${INPUT}"
   129→        git push -f origin "$BRANCH"
   130→
   131→        # Build PR body
   132→        BODY="Update \`${INPUT}\` input.
   133→
   134→        **Diff**: [\`${SHORT_OLD}...${SHORT_NEW}\`](${COMPARE_URL})"
   135→
   136→        VERSION_TABLE="${{ steps.versions.outputs.table }}"
   137→        if [ -n "$VERSION_TABLE" ]; then
   138→          BODY="${BODY}
   139→
   140→        ## Package Versions
   141→
   142→        ${VERSION_TABLE}"
   143→        fi
   144→
   145→        # Create or update PR
   146→        EXISTING_PR=$(gh pr list --head "$BRANCH" --state open --json number --jq '.[0].number' 2>/dev/null || true)
   147→
   148→        if [ -n "$EXISTING_PR" ] && [ "$EXISTING_PR" != "null" ]; then
   149→          echo "Updating existing PR #${EXISTING_PR}"
   150→          gh pr edit "$EXISTING_PR" --body "$BODY"
   151→        else
   152→          echo "Creating new PR"
   153→          gh pr create \
   154→            --title "flake.lock: update ${INPUT}" \
   155→            --body "$BODY" \
   156→            --head "$BRANCH"
   157→        fi
   158→
   159→        # Enable auto-merge
   160→        PR_NUMBER=$(gh pr list --head "$BRANCH" --state open --json number --jq '.[0].number')
   161→        if [ -n "$PR_NUMBER" ] && [ "$PR_NUMBER" != "null" ]; then
   162→          gh pr merge "$PR_NUMBER" --auto --squash
   163→        fi
   164→      env:
   165→        GH_TOKEN: ${{ inputs.token }}
   166→
```

```
     1→name: Update Flake Inputs (Reusable)
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  workflow_call:
     5→    inputs:
     6→      exclude-inputs:
     7→        description: Comma-separated list of inputs to exclude
     8→        type: string
     9→        default: ""
    10→      include-inputs:
    11→        description: Comma-separated list of inputs to include (if set, only these)
    12→        type: string
    13→        default: ""
    14→      skip-delay:
    15→        description: Skip random delay between updates
    16→        type: boolean
    17→        default: false
    18→      minimum-release-age-days:
    19→        description: Minimum age in days for the release commit
    20→        type: number
    21→        default: 0
    22→    secrets:
    23→      FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_ID:
    24→        required: true
    25→      FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:
    26→        required: true
    27→
    28→jobs:
    29→  discover:
    30→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    31→    outputs:
    32→      matrix: ${{ steps.discover.outputs.matrix }}
    33→    steps:
    34→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    35→      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix
    36→      - id: discover
    37→        uses: ./.github/actions/discover-flake-inputs
    38→        with:
    39→          exclude-inputs: ${{ inputs.exclude-inputs }}
    40→          include-inputs: ${{ inputs.include-inputs }}
    41→          skip-delay: ${{ inputs.skip-delay }}
    42→
    43→  update:
    44→    needs: discover
    45→    if: ${{ fromJSON(needs.discover.outputs.matrix).include[0] != null }}
    46→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    47→    strategy:
    48→      fail-fast: false
    49→      matrix: ${{ fromJSON(needs.discover.outputs.matrix) }}
    50→    steps:
    51→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    52→
    53→      - id: setup-bot
    54→        uses: ./.github/actions/setup-git-bot
    55→        with:
    56→          app-id: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_ID }}
    57→          app-private-key: ${{ secrets.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
    58→          app-slug: ${{ vars.FLAKE_UPDATER_APP_SLUG }}
    59→
    60→      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    61→        with:
    62→          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
    63→
    64→      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-nix
    65→
    66→      - uses: ./.github/actions/update-flake-input
    67→        with:
    68→          input: ${{ matrix.input }}
    69→          owner: ${{ matrix.owner }}
    70→          repo: ${{ matrix.repo }}
    71→          token: ${{ steps.setup-bot.outputs.token }}
    72→          skip-delay: ${{ matrix.skip_delay }}
    73→          minimum-release-age-days: ${{ inputs.minimum-release-age-days }}
    74→
    75→  summary:
    76→    needs: [discover, update]
    77→    if: always()
    78→    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04-arm
    79→    steps:
    80→      - run: |
    81→          echo "## Update Summary" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
    82→          echo "- Discover: ${{ needs.discover.result }}" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
    83→          echo "- Update: ${{ needs.update.result }}" >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"
    84→
